declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t1021 = call ptr @"example.com/stdtest/io.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t1023 = alloca i32
  %t1022 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t1021, ptr %t1023, ptr null)
  %t1024 = icmp ne i32 %t1022, 0
  br i1 %t1024, label %main.async.fail.104, label %main.async.ok.104
main.async.fail.104:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.104:
  %t1025 = load i32, ptr %t1023
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t1025
}
